Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [152] [JANUS: Foreseeing Latent Risk for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19913)
*Yuan Xiong,Linji Hao,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Janus，一种面向长时程工具使用智能体安全的前瞻式防护框架，并训练守卫模型 Vanguard 在危险动作执行前进行拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全方法多偏向内容审核或对已发生/即将发生的显性违规进行判断，难以处理工具使用智能体在长时程任务中由部分轨迹逐步累积、延迟显现的操作风险。因此需要一种能基于早期轨迹预判未来风险并提前干预的安全机制。

Method: Janus 通过多智能体仿真合成多样化智能体轨迹，并训练一个共享策略完成两个耦合任务：一是 anticipation task，用于从已观察到的轨迹前缀预测与安全相关的未来；二是 adjudication task，结合当前轨迹前缀和预测未来判断是否安全。两者通过 CoAA-RL 联合优化，使预测结果不仅准确，而且对下游安全裁决有用。最终得到的守卫模型 Vanguard 在动作执行前阻断不安全行为。

Result: 在四个智能体安全基准上，Vanguard 相比基线守卫模型将平均防护能力提升 15.9 个百分点，同时将良性任务完成率提升 5.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向未来风险的预测与安全裁决联合训练可以更有效地应对长时程智能体操作失败，在提升安全性的同时减少对正常任务执行的干扰。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [153] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域MDP中基于根节点的秩依赖风险目标规划，提出ERQDP，在避免场景树枚举和采样的情况下，通过秩-分位数替代目标、精确动态规划、PMF精确评估与anytime细化机制获得带证书的近似最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根节点风险目标对总回报分布施加秩依赖函数，目标关于回报分布非线性，通常破坏Bellman最优性；直接用场景树枚举求解计算不可行，因此需要一种可扩展且能给出误差证书的规划方法。

Method: 提出ERQDP：先构造并求解秩-分位数替代目标，可用精确动态规划求解；再在离散化回报网格上通过动态规划计算策略总回报PMF并精确评估候选策略，同时给出显式舍入误差界；最后在anytime循环中不断细化替代目标，并报告目标函数的上下界gap作为证书。

Result: 在测试基准上，ERQDP能够返回带证书的解或明确的剩余gap；支持快速风险参数扫描并显著提升运行效率；同时适用于风险规避和风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP为有限时域MDP下非线性秩依赖根风险目标提供了一种无需枚举、无需采样且可认证的求解框架，在精度、效率和风险偏好适应性之间取得了较好平衡。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [154] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOF-Sleuth：一个用于审计 MOF 晶体学信息文件（CIF）的强化学习引导智能体，通过确定性证据提取与推理模块结合，实现错误检测、归因和证据支撑解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型 MOF 数据库依赖 CIF 文件支持模拟、筛选和机器学习，但细微化学与结构错误会影响下游结果。现有验证器或机器学习方法多给出固定检查、粗粒度标签或分数，缺乏细粒度、证据支撑的解释；直接用 LLM 审计 CIF 又因证据隐含在原子位点、几何、连接性、占位和电荷等计算中而成本高且不可靠。

Method: 作者构建 MOF-Sleuth，包含两个模块：确定性的 Forensic Lab 从 CIF 中提取组成、几何、连接性、占位、配位和电荷等化学证据；Sleuth 推理引擎基于这些证据生成错误类型、二分类判定和证据支撑解释。进一步采用奖励引导强化学习，将工具测量结果转化为解释级监督，不仅奖励最终答案，也奖励引用的化学证据和由证据支持的诊断。作者还提出 Chemically Grounded Diagnosis（Chem-GD）指标，评估正确诊断是否由事实性、相关的 CIF 衍生证据支撑。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，MOF-Sleuth 在 LLM 方法和 MOF 专用机器学习方法中取得领先表现，提升了错误检测能力、归因能力以及基于证据的解释质量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将确定性化学证据提取与强化学习引导的语言推理相结合，可以显著增强 CIF 审计的可靠性、可解释性和细粒度诊断能力，为 MOF 数据库质量控制和下游计算任务提供更可信的自动化工具。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [155] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SenWorld：一种基于物理世界与事件溯源的确定性数字孪生模拟系统，用于生成隐私安全、可复现且带构造性真值标签的智能手机个人助手评测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助手需要基于长期个人数据进行推理，但真实设备轨迹含有高度隐私信息，难以共享；同时评测需要上下文丰富且答案已知的数据，传统人工标注或 LLM 评审可能引入不确定性与偏差。

Method: SenWorld 构建包含真实地图、天气、节假日和网络数据的模拟世界，让多个 persona 度过完整一天；系统以事件溯源方式记录可观测信号，并保存完整系统快照。评测标签不是事后标注生成，而是通过指向已有记录的快照指针固定。作者使用北京场景下的 16 个 persona 进行实验，并将生成数据投影为 717 个评测案例。

Result: 生成数据在类别分布上接近留出的真实用户基准，JSD 为 0.070；通信记录的日节律分布 JSD 低于 0.1，但生成记录长度仍短于真实记录。无脚本交互下，persona 形成了完全互惠的对话子图和差异化行为模式。对生产级手机助手的测试发现 78 个失败案例，主要集中在通话和短信记录检索，联系人、日程和闹钟任务未失败。

Conclusion: SenWorld 提供了一条隐私安全、可复现、分布经过检验的个人助手评测数据生成路径，其答案标签由系统构造过程固定，并可用快照指针验证失败原因，避免依赖 LLM judge。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [156] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoThink通过自剪枝训练减少冗余推理步骤，并通过偏好优化学习“从错到对”的顿悟式推理，从而在降低推理token消耗的同时提升大推理模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在解题时常因重复验证而“过度思考”，导致推理成本高。现有加速方法难以细粒度区分有益推理步骤与冗余步骤，可能在压缩推理过程时损害模型能力。

Method: 提出EvoThink框架，包含两部分：1）Self-Pruning Training（SPT）：无监督地迭代剪除冗余推理步骤，并用更简洁的轨迹进行自训练；2）Aha-Moment Preference Optimization（AMPO）：借鉴遗传算法，识别有价值的失败尝试，合成“从错误到正确”的顿悟时刻数据，并通过偏好优化让模型内化该推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上的实验显示，EvoThink显著减少推理阶段的token使用，同时提升LRM的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink表明，通过细粒度去除冗余验证并鼓励探索新推理路径，可以兼顾推理效率与推理性能，为缓解大推理模型过度思考提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [157] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文批评当前 AI 将 Transformer 作为跨模态统一模板的做法，主张借鉴神经科学中的结构异质性，构建由不同归纳偏置模块组成的异构拓扑网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，现代 AI 架构趋向于把同一种 Transformer 结构重复扩展到文本、图像和语音等任务，但大脑皮层的证据显示不同认知功能对应不同的细胞结构与层级组织。因此，论文试图指出当前统一架构范式可能不是风格选择，而是结构性错误。

Method: 论文采用概念性与功能主义分析：对比神经科学中的皮层细胞构筑证据、CNN 的局部感受野与层级结构经验、Transformer 的硬件适配历史以及 MoE 的同质专家划分，并将 Transformer 类比为海马结构的功能类似物，而非通用皮层模型。

Result: 论文得出观点：Transformer 的成功部分源于硬件与工程路径依赖，而非其作为通用智能结构的原则性优越；MoE 也未真正实现结构多样性；统一模板在听觉、执行控制、工作记忆等任务上可能缺乏相应的结构归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 论文提出 Heterogeneous Topological Network，即系统之系统的异构模块化架构。其核心主张是在训练前基于任务计算需求和结构证据设计模块与接口，而不是训练后再从行为中反推架构。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [158] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GSEM框架，用图结构经验记忆复用历史协调经验，以提升动态制造中多智能体在扰动下的快速适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态制造环境常受机器故障、紧急订单到达和加工时间变化等扰动影响，需要多智能体系统高效协调。现有多智能体强化学习方法通常将每次扰动视为独立事件，未充分利用历史协调经验，导致适应效率不足。

Method: 提出Graph-Structured Experiential Memory（GSEM）框架，将历史协调片段编码为异构关系图，表示任务依赖、机器状态和智能体协作模式；当新扰动发生时，利用基于图神经网络的检索机制查找结构相似的历史片段，并据此进行策略适应。

Result: 在动态柔性作业车间调度基准和三类扰动场景中，相比最强的记忆增强基线，GSEM使完工时间降低4.1%–10.0%，适应时间减少33%–38%；扰动频率越高，优势越明显。消融实验和跨扰动迁移实验验证了图结构编码、相似性检索及跨扰动泛化能力的重要性。

Conclusion: GSEM能够通过结构化地存储与检索历史协调经验，显著提升多智能体系统在动态制造扰动下的调度性能和适应速度，并具备较好的跨扰动迁移潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [159] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK 将知识图谱、符号规则挖掘与 LP^MLN 概率推理结合，用于在分布变化和不确定性下提升分类任务的可解释性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习分类模型依赖数据中的统计模式，面对数据分布变化时性能下降，并且难以有效融合先验知识；因此需要一种能处理不确定性、适应知识演化且具备可解释性的分类框架。

Method: 提出 CLARK 框架：从 CACTUS 生成的知识图谱出发，将图结构转换为 LP^MLN 程序；随后通过符号学习器迭代提出候选规则，并利用概率权重学习对规则进行校准，从而支持不确定性推理并反向优化知识图谱结构。

Result: 在两个医学数据集上评估规则质量和下游分类性能，结果显示 CLARK 能提升分类表现，并产生更具泛化性的推理结果。

Conclusion: CLARK 提供了一种原则化的闭环学习方法，可构建自适应、可解释且知识驱动的分类模型，适合处理不确定和动态变化的信息环境。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [160] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向 IT 运维自动修复的安全决策框架，将“是否干预”纳入风险约束优化，在降低错误修复率的同时保持修复效果并减少人工升级负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 IT-Ops 中，错误修复的代价可能高于不采取行动；现有自动化修复系统偏重生成修复动作，而缺乏对干预必要性与安全性的系统性判断，往往依赖人工审批兜底。

Method: 作者将安全修复建模为受风险约束的干预决策问题，并形式化为 CMDP，以最大化修复成功率且约束错误修复率；提出由影响范围、可逆性、认知不确定性组成的三维风险分解；设计上下文自适应 HITL 门控机制，根据值班负载与业务关键性动态决定是否升级人工；策略基于历史事件日志离线学习。

Result: 在 Train Ticket 微服务基准、Chaos Mesh 故障注入和 RCAEval 对齐故障分类上，相比强 runbook 基线，框架将错误修复率降低 39%，修复成功率提升 2.5 个百分点；相比固定阈值变体，人工升级负载降低 17%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将自动修复从动作生成问题扩展为带风险约束的干预决策问题，可以实现更可控、更可解释且更节省人工带宽的安全 IT 运维自动化。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [161] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC 是一个用于先进工艺节点物理设计中块级 DRC 自动修复的技能演化框架，通过 LLM 代理、局部验证工具和经验知识库持续改进修复策略，在基准测试中显著减少 DRV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进节点物理设计中，DRC 收敛仍是主要瓶颈。尽管详细布线器具备规则感知能力，仍会残留 DRV，需要大量人工 ECO 迭代。自动化修复困难在于必须同时处理复杂几何交互、保持电路连通性，并避免引入新的违规。

Method: 论文提出 EvoDRC：先从无关参考设计中蒸馏知识，初始化不同金属层或版图层的修复技能；随后将目标版图分解为有边界的局部修复区域，并为每个区域分配 LLM 修复代理。代理利用局部 DRC 分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具获得反馈，提出并验证修改。修复操作及其引起的 DRV 变化被存入知识数据库，用于持续演化修复技能。

Result: 在 DAC26 DRC Benchmark 的七个块级设计上，EvoDRC 相比所报告基线实现了 73.5% 的整体违规减少。

Conclusion: EvoDRC 表明，将 LLM 代理与可追踪的版图分析工具、经验数据库和技能演化机制结合，可以有效提升块级 DRC 自动修复能力，减少人工 ECO 负担，并推动先进节点物理设计中的 DRC 收敛自动化。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [162] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对差分约束 x−y≤d 的边界一致全局传播器，将所有差分约束统一处理，而非逐个传播，从而显著提升有限域求解中的传播效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分约束可通过最短路算法高效完成可满足性与蕴含检测，但传统有限域约束传播通常把每个差分约束作为独立传播器处理，虽保证完备性但可能效率低下。作者希望将图算法和差分逻辑理论求解器的优势引入有限域传播，构建更高效的全局传播机制。

Method: 作者设计了一个边界一致的全局差分约束传播器，同时处理整个差分约束集合；借鉴 SAT modulo theory 中差分约束理论求解器的思想，但针对约束传播器的需求进行适配。关键技术包括全局检测与传播差分约束蕴含关系，并为 lazy clause generation 求解器中的传播生成解释。

Result: 实验表明，相比标准的逐约束传播方法，全局处理差分约束能够带来实质性的性能提升，减少冗余传播并加速求解过程。

Conclusion: 差分约束不应仅作为多个局部约束分散处理；通过全局传播器结合最短路/差分逻辑推理，并提供可用于惰性子句生成的解释机制，可以显著改善有限域约束求解性能。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [163] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开放权重大语言模型 google/gemma-4-E4B-it 是否真正表征并使用材料科学中的物理机制，而不仅是给出正确答案。结果表明，机制信息在模型内部以概念可读性、状态间受控变换、以及对答案的因果控制三种可分离形式存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够回答科学问题，但正确输出并不能证明模型理解或使用了支配物理规律。作者希望区分表层文本相关性、数值比较等替代解释，与模型内部是否存在可解释、可操控、具有因果作用的材料科学机制表征。

Method: 作者结合直接词汇读出与 Jacobian 词汇读出、无选项的隐藏状态几何分析、包含 60 条物理定律的反事实基准，以及因果干预实验。具体包括：在材料描述上检验概念读出一致性；用目标无关词集盲识别机制家族；构建 72 个提示的机制邻域分析；通过精确图审计排除数值比较解释；再设计仅反转物理输入方向的配对提示，检验隐藏状态变化是否遵循相应构成律；最后通过双向干预和状态 patching 测试内部表征对答案概率的因果影响。

Result: 在 50 个留出材料描述中，三个独立拟合的 Jacobian lenses 复现了概念排序，且两类读出得到的目标无关词集可盲识别 10 个机制家族中的 9 个。72-prompt 基准显示机制特异的隐藏状态邻域，但图审计发现该现象同样可由数值比较解释。进一步的方向反转实验中，状态变换能在 60 个冻结关系上区分直接律、物理中性关系与反向律，并在 40 个方向性定律中正确定向 39 个；词汇控制接近随机。12 个匹配案例中的双向干预均能按物理合理方向移动答案概率，反事实状态 patching 还能跨机制与答案格式转移相反决策信号。

Conclusion: 材料科学物理关系在模型中的体现，不仅存在于静态隐藏状态的概念可读性中，更明显地体现在受控输入变化引发的隐藏状态变换中；部分内部表征还对工程答案具有因果控制作用。因此，评估科学型大语言模型时，应关注动态状态变化与因果干预，而不能仅依赖答案正确率或静态表征聚类。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [164] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG通过程序化记忆管理完整结构化交互日志，并让编码型智能体高效检索历史，从而提升LLM智能体在长时程探索任务中的表现，同时显著降低上下文token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务需要持续感知、推理与探索，但LLM智能体在ARC-AGI-3等持续学习基准上表现有限。核心瓶颈之一是上下文管理：保存更多环境信息有助于完整性，但会导致相关信息检索困难、token成本升高。

Method: 提出PRO-LONG，一个面向长时程探索场景的最小化上下文管理框架。它保留完整且结构化的交互日志，并利用编码智能体的能力，以程序化方式搜索和加载历史信息，从而在信息保真度与检索效率之间取得平衡。

Result: 在完整ARC-AGI-3公开游戏集上，PRO-LONG相较基础编码智能体在前沿模型上平均提升18.0个百分点；达到或超过专用agent harness的SOTA水平，最高76.1% pass@1；同时token使用量减少4.2到5.8倍。使用Fable 5时达到97.4% best@2，总成本为1750美元。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对长时程LLM智能体，程序化记忆与结构化完整日志是一种有效且经济的上下文管理方案，可在保持历史信息完整性的同时提升检索效率和任务表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [165] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRUST-ESD，一个面向企业战略决策支持的不确定性感知、风险校准与治理合规框架；相比强基线，在风险调整效用、风险暴露、CVaR、校准误差、解释保真度和治理合规性上均有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策需要 AI 系统不仅具备较高预测准确性，还要能量化不确定性、控制下行风险、提供可解释依据，并满足治理与合规要求。传统仅按最大期望效用选择行动的方法容易忽略可靠性、风险暴露和政策约束。

Method: TRUST-ESD 对可行的反事实策略进行评估，结合预测效用估计、保形不确定性校准、基于 CVaR 的下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、policy-as-code 治理验证、可解释性机制和人工监督，以综合平衡价值、可靠性、风险与合规。

Result: 实验显示，相比强不确定性感知基线，TRUST-ESD 将风险调整效用提升 7.95%，风险暴露降低 23.22%，CVaR 降低 23.78%，校准误差降低 13.89%，解释保真度提升 10.90%，治理合规性提升 9.76%，同时保持有竞争力的预测准确率。

Conclusion: TRUST-ESD 表明，将不确定性校准、下行风险度量、风险记忆、解释机制和治理验证联合纳入企业决策流程，可以提升企业战略决策的可信性、稳健性与合规性。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [166] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MGT-B，一种面向量化小型自回归推理模型的推理时外部监控与回退重解码控制器；在严格的时间审计集上仅显示非显著的准确率提升，在更宽松历史集上提升更明显但仅具探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化小型自回归推理模型在生成过程中可能陷入冗长、重复或低效轨迹，而常规推理时计算分配通常不根据轨迹演化状态动态干预。作者希望通过监测不确定性与退化特征，识别问题轨迹并进行选择性修复。

Method: 基于早期 token 级 e-CUSUM 控制器，提出 MGT-B：用重叠窗口提取预采样不确定性与退化特征，映射为位置条件经验尾概率；累积混合 betting factors 并采用 CUSUM 式重置；触发告警后估计回滚点，恢复 token 与 KV-cache 状态，并执行受约束重解码。实验包含 240 对 chronology-audit 集和 467 对 historical-coverage 集。

Result: 在 240 对审计集上，准确率从 82/240 提升至 88/240，增加 2.50 个百分点；13 个修正、7 个退化，McNemar 精确检验 p=0.2632，bootstrap 95% 区间为 [-1.25,+6.25]，不显著。在 467 对历史覆盖集上，准确率从 146/467 提升至 167/467，增加 4.50 个百分点，McNemar p=0.000753；但该集包含阈值选择前或期间可见的样本，因此仅作为探索性估计。467 对中 316 个未告警输出与 vanilla 完全一致，151 个告警轨迹中有 29 个修正和 8 个退化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，MGT-B 在所研究的 MATH-500 场景中可能具备选择性监控与修复能力，但当前证据不是确证性的；经验因子也未被证明是有效的 e-process 或 e-detector，因此不能宣称其带来一般性或理论认证的推理能力提升。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [167] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoTRE 提出一种由四类异构推理代理与任务自适应聚合层组成的集成框架，用于提升大语言模型在复杂长程推理中的鲁棒性与准确率，并在多个高难基准上取得较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在需要长程规划、迭代纠错、处理新抽象概念或严格领域约束的复杂推理任务中表现不稳定；传统单一路径提示方法容易脆弱失效，因此需要更鲁棒的多视角推理架构。

Method: 论文提出 PoTRE（Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles），将推理解耦为四个异构代理：对抗式精炼代理、层级战略规划代理、谱系搜索代理和直接链式代理；随后通过任务自适应聚合层进行候选选择、语义综合或神经符号验证，以生成最终全局解。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-2、HLE 和 PRBench Finance 三个前沿基准上评估；其中在 HLE 上达到 49.92% 的准确率，声称超过此前最佳官方成绩，并且相较大规模同质基线使用相近或更少推理 token。

Conclusion: 该摘要表明，异构推理集成比单一推理流更适合复杂任务，可在不显著增加推理成本的情况下提升推理性能与稳定性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出以重构分数评估自然语言自编码器解释的忠实性存在结构性缺陷：高重构不代表具体断言真实；提出审计协议与 RECAP，使指定内部内容可由独立探针验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用“解释能否重构隐藏激活”来衡量解释是否忠实，但这种测试可能只捕捉语义大意，对解释中的具体错误断言不敏感，甚至允许模型通过私有编码或误导性措辞获得高分。

Method: 作者分析已发布的 Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer，并在合成真值环境中检验标准训练方案；提出 grounded-vs-true cross 与 evaluator swap 两种审计协议；进一步提出 RECAP，即在目标模型训练时共同训练线性辅助预测头，使指定内容保持可解码，并用独立探针对解释中的真假断言进行验证。

Result: 在 Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer 上，解释重构显著高于随机水平，但仅约 2% 的具体断言会影响重构，说明分数主要反映大意。在合成设置中，标准方案 5/5 次产生协同适应的私有编码，且不改变目标模型的修补无效。RECAP 沙盒模型中，新 verbalizer 能真实表述指定内容且私有码消失，代价仅 +0.001 nat。在 Pythia-160M 上，指定内容变得可由探针可靠解码，新 verbalizer 部分传达该内容；RECAP 下独立探针对真伪断言区分 AUC 达 0.96，对抗编辑下仍为 0.95，而对照探针接近随机。

Conclusion: 高重构分数不能证明自然语言解释中个别断言的忠实性。RECAP 通过让指定内部内容可被独立探针检查，降低模型用可操纵文本解释欺骗评估的风险，为解释可靠性与安全审计提供更稳健路径。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [169] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SoftReason，一种用于在潜在感知事实与知识提供谓词上进行可微演绎推理的神经-软符号架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号系统在感知模块与符号推理模块之间存在离散接口，导致梯度无法端到端传播；同时许多推理任务的前提并非显式符号，而是需要从高维输入中推断，并结合知识图谱或规则定义提供的谓词与证据。

Method: SoftReason 将演绎状态表示为候选常量和谓词上的局部软解释张量；感知模块生成概率化基础事实，知识图谱三元组作为高置信软证据注入。核心是学习式可微 immediate-consequence operator 提升：利用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体谓词混合，对可能见证变量进行聚合，生成查询条件化头事实，并通过单调概率 OR 更新解释。

Result: 在知识感知视觉问答任务中实例化该框架，展示其能够统一支持端到端感知 grounding、知识图谱证据注入和可微演绎闭包。

Conclusion: SoftReason 通过软解释张量和可微闭包更新弥合感知与演绎之间的梯度断层，为结合高维感知输入、知识证据和规则推理的端到端训练提供了一种可行架构。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [MC-BRIDGE: A Modular Receiver-Chain Simulation Framework for OECT-Based Molecular Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19502)
*Hongbin Ni,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 MC-BRIDGE：一个用于 OECT 分子通信接收机链路设计与评估的模块化框架，将分子释放、扩散、有限面积观测、随机结合、OECT 转导、噪声、校准与电荷域检测贯通建模，并显示几何、噪声协方差、校准方式和记忆效应会显著改变接收机性能结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OECT 基分子通信接收机需要同时考虑分子传输、受体结合、器件电流响应、噪声、校准和检测决策。已有分析若忽略有限面积观测、通道相关噪声、受体/传输记忆或校准流程，可能导致对符号错误率和接收机预算需求的误判。因此需要一个端到端、模块化且可复现实验评估的统一框架。

Method: 作者构建 MC-BRIDGE 框架，连接释放、胞外扩散、有限面积被动观测、随机结合与 OECT 转导，并在独立电气时间网格上生成序列级多通道有色噪声。框架支持控制通道参考、电荷积分检测，以及 MoSK、CSK 和混合判决；通过统一模块接口估计 SER、解码符号互信息并分析 ISI。其评估流程包含自适应预算搜索、独立种子保留测试、每个工况单独阈值校准，并比较被动场无记忆测试与保留传输/受体记忆的情形。

Result: 在标称距离下，有限面积被动观测器仅保留中心点决策电荷幅值的 27.5%。控制通道参考是否有效取决于选择性通道与控制通道噪声/响应的相关性。经过自适应搜索后，作者用与搜索和校准不重合的随机种子保留记录测试所选预算及次低预算，从而给出满足 SER 目标的最小预算的测试网格上界。若采用重置受体占据、排除 ISI 的被动场测试，性能结论较乐观；若保留传输与受体记忆，则 SER 显著升高。

Conclusion: MC-BRIDGE 表明，OECT 分子通信接收机的性能评估不能只看理想化传输或单点响应，必须联合考虑观测几何、通道协方差、阈值校准策略以及传输/受体记忆导致的 ISI。否则对控制参考、检测预算和 SER 的判断可能发生根本变化。

Abstract: Organic electrochemical transistor (OECT)-based molecular communication (MC) receivers connect transport and binding to device current, noise, calibration, and detection. We present MC-BRIDGE (Molecular Communication Bioelectronic Receiver-chain Integrated Design and Guided Evaluation), a modular framework linking release, extracellular diffusion, finite-area observation, stochastic binding, and OECT transduction to charge-domain detection. On an independent electrical time grid, it generates sequence-wide multichannel colored noise, performs control-channel referencing and charge integration, and supports molecule-shift-keying (MoSK), concentration-shift-keying (CSK), and Hybrid decisions. Through common module interfaces, it estimates symbol error rate (SER) and decoded-symbol mutual information and analyzes inter-symbol interference (ISI). At nominal separation, the passive finite-area observer retains 27.5 percent of the center-point decision-charge magnitude, while correlation between the selective and control channels determines whether control referencing helps. After adaptive search, held-out records with seeds disjoint from search and calibration test the selected and next-lower budgets, yielding a tested-grid upper bound on the minimum budget meeting the SER target. This passive-field test resets receptor occupancy, excludes ISI, and calibrates thresholds on separate records at each operating point. Retaining transport and receptor memory instead produces high SER. Thus, geometry, covariance, calibration, and memory can change receiver conclusions.

</details>


### [171] [FullPASS: Geometry Optimization for Full-Duplex Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19546)
*Morteza Barzegar Astanjin,Seyed Mohammad Azimi-Abarghouyi,Thomas Eriksson,Mikko Valkama*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出FullPASS：一种基于双平行波导的带内全双工 pinching-antenna system 架构，通过联合选择发射/接收激活单元，在控制自干扰泄漏的同时最大化双向和频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PASS需要在同一时频资源中高效支持上下行通信，但带内全双工会引入发射到接收链路的自干扰；同时，候选pinching单元的联合激活选择带来非凸组合优化难题。因此需要一种能兼顾频谱效率、自干扰约束与计算复杂度的架构和算法。

Method: 构建包含下行、上行及发射到接收自干扰路径的几何信道模型，纳入自由空间传播、波导内传播相位与衰减、以及上游已激活单元带来的额外衰减。将联合发射/接收单元激活建模为二进制优化问题，目标是最大化双向和频谱效率并满足自干扰阈值约束。提出两阶段求解算法：第一阶段基于简化传播模型，使用相位锚定二阶锥松弛与确定性舍入生成候选二进制激活模式；第二阶段在完整传播模型下，采用交替best-improvement局部搜索，并通过添加、移除、交换操作优化激活集合。

Result: 仿真表明，在13×13和15×15候选网格上，所提方法的平均和频谱效率与穷举搜索相比差距在0.95%以内；在15×15网格上，平均运行时间比穷举搜索降低超过一个数量级，并可扩展到更大的候选集合。

Conclusion: FullPASS通过双波导全双工架构和高效联合激活算法，在接近最优性能的同时显著降低搜索复杂度，是一种适用于大规模PASS候选单元部署的高效全双工通信方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes FullPASS, an in-band full-duplex architecture for pinching-antenna systems (PASSs) based on two parallel waveguides. The FullPASS transceiver simultaneously communicates with a single full-duplex user terminal over the same time-frequency resource: the transmit waveguide delivers the downlink signal, while the receive waveguide collects the uplink signal. Candidate pinching elements are placed along both waveguides, and the FullPASS transceiver jointly selects the active transmit and receive elements. We derive a geometry-based channel model for the downlink, uplink, and transmit-to-receive self-interference paths, including free-space propagation, in-waveguide propagation phase and attenuation, and the attenuation caused by upstream activated elements along each waveguide. The joint activation problem is formulated as a binary optimization that maximizes the bidirectional sum spectral efficiency while keeping the self-interference leakage at the FullPASS receiver below a prescribed threshold. To solve the resulting nonconvex combinatorial problem, we develop a two-stage algorithm. The first stage uses phase-anchored second-order-cone relaxations and deterministic rounding to generate binary trial activation patterns under a simplified propagation model. The second stage applies alternating best-improvement local search with add, remove, and swap operations evaluated under the full propagation model. Simulations show that the proposed method achieves an average sum spectral efficiency within 0.95% of exhaustive search on both the 13-by-13 and 15-by-15 candidate grids. On the 15-by-15 grid, it reduces the average runtime by more than one order of magnitude relative to exhaustive search and remains applicable to substantially larger candidate sets.

</details>


### [172] [New lower bounds for binary constant-weight codes: $A(23,6,10)\geq 2979$ and $A(24,6,10)\geq 4214$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19550)
*Christian Lysenstoeen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文构造了新的二进制常重码显式下界，改进并超过若干既有记录，同时提供可复现实验材料与验证工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是提升常重码函数 A(n,d,w) 的已知下界，特别是针对长度 23、24、最小距离 6、特定重量的困难实例；同时验证和替代早期已遗失代码列表的历史界。

Method: 采用坐标分解策略：固定一半坐标为已知码，另一半从完整交叉兼容候选池中选择，并使用 CHILS 求解最大权独立集；此外用两类精确求解器验证既有码的插入极大性与小规模交换不可改进性，并分析素数阶置换不变码。

Result: 得到 A(23,6,10)≥2979、A(24,6,10)≥4214、A(23,6,11)≥3539、A(24,6,8)≥1855；其中前两个超过已有显式构造和历史界。还证明若干对称类型不可行或给出上界，例如 5+1^18 类型上界为 499。

Conclusion: 论文通过组合构造、启发式优化与精确验证相结合，提升了多个常重码参数的公开下界，并以代码文件、验证器、模型与日志增强了结果的可复现性和可信度。

Abstract: Let $A(n,d,w)$ denote the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code of length $n$, minimum distance $d$, and weight $w$. We construct explicit codes proving $A(23,6,10)\ge 2979$ and $A(24,6,10)\ge 4214$. These improve the best surviving explicit codes of sizes 2969 and 4174 and surpass the corresponding 1990 bounds 2970 and 4200 of Brouwer, Shearer, Sloane and Smith, whose code listings were lost. We also obtain $A(23,6,11)\ge 3539$ and $A(24,6,8)\ge 1855$. All four bounds are now listed in Brouwer's online table. The constructions use a coordinate decomposition in which one half is fixed to a known code and the complementary half is selected from its full cross-compatible pool using CHILS for maximum-weight independent set. For the 2969-word $A(23,6,10)$ incumbent, exact computations with two solver families prove insertion maximality and exclude every improving exchange deleting at most three codewords. We also analyze codes invariant under prime-order permutations: several cycle types are excluded exactly, the $5+1^{18}$ type has upper bound 499, and reproducible heuristic saturation evidence is reported for the remaining types, with $13+1^{10}$ left open. Code files, an independent validator, model descriptions, and computational logs are released.

</details>


### [173] [Combinatorial Capacity Bounds for the $q$-ary Deletion Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19559)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Thinh Nguyen,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 q 元删除信道的有限块长容量界，通过模式计数标量 N_n(x,y) 建立转移概率分解、归一化恒等式与均匀输入下的精确互信息表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 删除信道容量通常难以精确刻画，尤其在有限块长下。作者希望利用删除模式到输出序列的计数结构，得到可验证的概率归一化、熵表达式以及容量上下界，从而为 q 元删除信道提供更明确的解析结果。

Method: 引入 N_n(x,y)，即把输入 x 通过删除子集映射到输出 y 的删除模式数量；证明关于 N_n 的两个求和恒等式，用于验证信道转移概率归一化，并在均匀输入分布下推导输出熵与互信息的闭式表达；进一步由互信息给出有限块容量夹逼界和小删除概率渐近下界，并通过小规模数值实验验证。

Result: 得到有限块容量界：(1-d)log2 q - h2(d) ≤ C_{q,n} ≤ (1-d)log2 q。均匀输入率的精确表达式为 (1/n)I_U(X;Y)=(1-d)log2 q + H_Bin(n,1-d)/n - h2(d)+Δ_n(d)/n，并推出认证下界 C_{q,n} ≥ (1-d)log2 q - h2(d)+Δ_n(d)/n。小 d 时得到 C_q(d) ≥ log2 q + d log2 d + O(d)，适用于 q≥2。数值实验在 n=3,5,10 与 q=2,3 下确认了这些界。

Conclusion: 该工作以模式计数为核心，为 q 元删除信道提供了结构化的有限块长分析框架，并导出了简洁的容量夹逼界、均匀输入互信息公式和小删除概率下界；其贡献偏理论推导，数值实验主要用于验证解析结果的一致性。

Abstract: We study the \(q\)-ary deletion channel via the pattern-count scalar \(N_n(x,y)\), the number of deletion subsets mapping \(x\inΣ_q^n\) to \(y\inΣ_q^k\), which factorizes the transition probability. Two sum identities on \(N_n\) certify stochastic normalization and, under uniform input, yield an exact closed-form output entropy. These give the finite-block capacity sandwich \( (1-d)\log_2 q-h_2(d)\;\le\; C_{q,n}\;\le\;(1-d)\log_2 q. \) The exact uniform-input rate is \( \frac{1}{n}I_U(X;Y) =(1-d)\log_2 q+\frac{1}{n}H_{\mathrm{Bin}}(n,1-d)-h_2(d)+\frac{Δ_n(d)}{n}, \) from which the simpler certified bound \( C_{q,n}\ge (1-d)\log_2 q-h_2(d)+\frac{Δ_n(d)}{n} \) follows. The small-\(d\) bound \(C_q(d)\ge\log_2 q+d\log_2 d+O(d)\) follows for all \(q\ge 2\). Numerical experiments at \(n=3,5,10\) and \(q=2,3\) confirm all bounds.

</details>


### [174] [Extinction Depth and q-ary Error-Correcting Codes for the Limited Permutation Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19566)
*Noam Ben Shimon,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究半径一有限置换信道中用于纠正/检测相邻不交叠换位错误的分组拼接码，提出“消亡深度”准则与有限状态验证方法，并给出新的显式码率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在该信道中，汉明距离不足以刻画可混淆性；已有针对分组拼接码的碰撞测试只是充分条件而非必要条件，可能漏掉有效码集。因此需要更精确、可验证且适用于所有长度的判定准则。

Method: 作者引入消亡深度，用于跟踪首块码字对在后续块扩展中尚未解决的混淆状态；证明若这些未解决状态在某个共同有限深度全部消亡，则可保证任意长度上的纠错性。进一步构造有限图/有限状态测试，并将理论扩展到检测问题和任意固定位移半径。

Result: 给出三元和五元字母表上的显式分组集合，码率分别超过 0.6777475 和 0.6694926；证明不存在与分组集合无关的统一深度界；给出线性精确族、验证若干二次公式范围，并建立多项式时间有限图测试。对增长字母表还得到归一化纠正损失界，并改进有限字母表上界；检测版本也获得全长度准则、精确走廊深度与更好的低字母表下界。

Conclusion: 消亡深度提供了比既有测试更强且仍可有限验证的纠错/检测判据，能认证此前无法认证的有效分组集合，并统一推广到更一般的固定半径有限置换信道。

Abstract: In the radius-one limited permutation channel, errors consist of disjoint adjacent transpositions. A correcting code must separate distinct codewords: their error balls may not contain a common received word. Hamming distance does not ensure this, because disjoint swaps can make words differing in many positions confusable. For block-concatenation codes, earlier work tested each possible collision only through the longer initial block. This sufficient condition is not necessary: we exhibit a valid ternary block set that it does not certify. We introduce extinction depth, which tracks unresolved first-block pairs through later block extensions, and prove that their extinction at one common finite horizon certifies correction at every length. The criterion gives explicit $q=3$ and $q=5$ block sets with rates above $0.6777475$ and $0.6694926$. No block-set-independent depth bound exists: we give an exact linear family, verify a quadratic formula for every $3\leq k\leq 60$, and derive a polynomial-time finite-graph test. For growing alphabets, the normalized correction loss lies between $\ln\varphi$ and $\ln(1.82560995)$, while explicit finite-length covers improve finite-alphabet upper bounds. We develop a parallel directed-extinction theory for detection, including an all-length block criterion, a set unresolved by the earlier test, and exact corridor depth $4k$. Stable type lifting yields optimal first-order loss $\sqrt{2}$, and weak-zigzag codes improve the $q=3,4$ lower bounds. Finally, window restriction, cancellation, and a bounded pending-input frontier extend the criterion and its finite-state verification to every fixed displacement radius $r$.

</details>


### [175] [Bounds and Limitations on Codes Achieving List Recovery Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19576)
*Joshua Brakensiek,Yeyuan Chen,Aaron Putterman,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究编码理论中的列表恢复问题，给出了紧的广义 Singleton 界，并证明基于 AEL 框架的显式构造方法存在无法突破特定列表恢复障碍的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表恢复刻画码字在坐标上的“分散性”，并广泛应用于复杂性理论、性质测试、压缩感知、流算法和密码学。已有工作虽能构造接近容量的显式码，但其最优列表恢复能力及构造框架的极限仍不清楚。

Method: 作者首先从信息论角度建立广义 Singleton bound，形式化给出在常数列表参数和大字母表条件下可达与不可达的码率阈值。其次，对 Alon–Edmonds–Luby（AEL）启发的一类近期构造进行元分析，抽象出统一的 AEL 框架，并在该框架内证明限制性结果。

Result: 对于常数 ℓ、L、ρ 和足够大的字母表，定义 R*=(L+1-ℓ)/L - ((L+1)/L)ρ，则存在码率 R*−ε 的 (ρ,ℓ,L) 列表可恢复码，但不存在码率 R*+ε 的此类码。另一个结果表明，任何 AEL 框架内的码都无法突破近期发现的加性码和线性码列表恢复障碍。

Conclusion: 本文同时给出了列表恢复的精确码率极限，并指出当前一大类显式最优构造方法的内在瓶颈，暗示若要构造更强的最优列表可恢复码，需要超越 AEL 类型框架的新技术。

Abstract: In coding theory, list recoverability is a fundamental concept which robustly captures how ``spread-out'' codewords are in a code. More formally, given a code $C \subseteq Σ^n$ and input lists $S_1, \dots, S_n \subseteq Σ$ of size at most $\ell$, list recoverability requires that there are at most $L$ codewords $c \in C$ such that $c_i \in S_i$ for at least $(1-ρ)n$ choices of $i \in [n]$. List recovery is an important question which has found applications in many areas, including complexity theory, property testing, compressed sensing, streaming algorithms, and cryptography.
  As our first main result, we establish a tight ``generalized singleton bound''. Formally, we show that for constant $\ell, L,ρ$ and sufficiently large alphabets $Σ$, if we define $R^*=\frac{L+1-\ell}{L}-\frac{L+1}{L}ρ$, it is possible for a $(ρ,\ell,L)$ list-recoverable code to have rate $R^*-ε$ but impossible to have rate $R^*+ε$. One direction of our result already directly generalizes and improves a weaker impossibility result due to Goldberg, Shangguan, and Tamo.
  For our second main result, we prove that there is a fundamental shortcoming in existing methods that aim to construct explicit, optimal list-recoverable codes. Indeed, recent work has constructed explicit codes achieving list-decoding capacity (along with other related properties) using a framework introduced in the work of Alon--Edmonds--Luby (AEL). We give a meta-analysis of such constructions by presenting an ``AEL framework'' which captures all such recent constructions in the literature. Within this framework, we show that no AEL-based code can break a recently-identified list-recovery barrier for additive and linear codes.

</details>


### [176] [Learning to Transmit: Volatility-Aware Predictive Communication for Energy-Efficient IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19590)
*John Kangethe,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向物联网的波动性感知预测通信框架，通过本地轻量预测与自适应阈值抑制冗余传输，在显著降低通信次数的同时保持较低重构误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网络中通信通常是主要能耗来源，而传感数据往往具有强时间相关性，许多测量值对接收端的信息增益有限。固定阈值或静态模型难以适应环境变化、季节性波动、部署差异以及长期非平稳性，因此需要一种无需人工调参、可随信号波动自适应调整的节能通信机制。

Method: 论文提出 ADAPTIVEML：每个传感器端维护轻量级机器学习预测器，仅当实际观测与预测值之间的残差超过与本地信号波动成比例的自适应阈值时才发送数据。该方法使用滚动信号变异性估计对预测误差进行归一化，从而适应动态环境。进一步提出 ADAPTIVEML-RLS，采用带指数遗忘的递归最小二乘在线学习，以应对传感器漂移和长期信号分布变化。

Result: 在三个异构真实数据集上进行实验，数据规模超过 240 万条观测，覆盖室外环境监测、室内无线传感网络和城市空气质量感知。相较周期传输、静态阈值抑制、ARIMA、Kalman 滤波、EMA 和 LMS 滤波等六类基线，ADAPTIVEML 最高减少 94.7% 的传输，同时保持 0.352°C 的重构误差；在漂移条件下，ADAPTIVEML-RLS 进一步降低 12%–18% 的重构误差，同时保持超过 93% 的传输削减。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于波动性感知的预测通信能够有效减少物联网设备通信能耗，并在环境变化和信号漂移下保持较好的数据重构精度与自适应能力，适合能效敏感的自适应物联网应用。

Abstract: Communication is the dominant source of energy consumption in Internet-of-Things (IoT) networks, yet many sensed measurements exhibit strong temporal correlations and provide little new information to the receiver. This paper introduces \textsc{ADAPTIVEML}, a volatility-aware predictive communication framework that enables IoT devices to intelligently decide when communication is necessary. Each sensor maintains a lightweight machine learning predictor and transmits only when the prediction residual exceeds an adaptive threshold proportional to the local signal volatility. By normalizing prediction errors using a rolling estimate of signal variability, the proposed transmission policy automatically adapts to changing environmental conditions, seasonal variations, and deployment-specific dynamics without manual threshold tuning. To address long-term non-stationarity, we further propose \textsc{ADAPTIVEML-RLS}, an online learning extension based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) with exponential forgetting, allowing continuous adaptation to sensor drift and evolving signal characteristics. Extensive experiments are conducted on three heterogeneous real-world datasets comprising more than 2.4 million sensor observations from outdoor environmental monitoring, indoor wireless sensor networks, and urban air-quality sensing. Compared with six representative baselines, including periodic transmission, static-threshold suppression, ARIMA, Kalman filtering, EMA, and LMS filtering, \textsc{ADAPTIVEML} achieves up to 94.7\% transmission reduction while maintaining a reconstruction error of 0.352$^\circ$C. \textsc{ADAPTIVEML-RLS} further reduces reconstruction error by 12--18\% under drift conditions while preserving transmission reduction above 93\%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of volatility-aware predictive communication for energy-efficient and adaptive IoT networks.

</details>


### [177] [CRB-Driven Beamforming and Trajectory Optimization for UAV-assisted ISAC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19609)
*Yi Yang,Qianqian Zhang,Huaxia Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究UAV辅助的ISAC系统，通过联合优化无人机轨迹与波束成形，在满足下行通信可靠性约束的同时提升基站对目标的感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线网络需要同时支持高精度感知与可靠通信。UAV具有可控移动性和自适应覆盖能力，可增强基站对目标的感知能力，但其轨迹、波束成形、功率、移动性和通信约束之间存在耦合优化难题。

Method: 以平均Cramér-Rao Bound（CRB）表征角到达估计的感知性能，构建联合UAV轨迹与波束成形优化问题。针对非凸性，采用零空间投影进行波束成形设计，并使用深度强化学习在离散时间尺度上优化UAV轨迹；每个时隙基于信道状态信息调整波束成形，以降低CRB并抑制对通信用户的干扰。

Result: 仿真结果表明，相比无UAV辅助的ISAC系统，所提方法使时间平均CRB降低超过10%；同时相比固定UAV轨迹和基于最大比传输的波束成形基准方案，获得更高的感知精度。

Conclusion: UAV辅助能够有效增强ISAC系统的感知性能；通过联合轨迹规划与波束成形设计，可在保障下行通信需求的同时显著提升目标角度估计精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system, where a UAV enhances the sensing capability of a base station (BS) towards a target while ensuring reliable communication towards a downlink user. This architecture is practically attractive for future wireless networks due to the UAV's controllable mobility and adaptive sensing coverage in wireless environments. The sensing performance is characterized by the average Cramér-Rao bound (CRB), which quantifies the minimum variance of the unbiased angle-of-arrival estimation. To enhance the sensing performance, the UAV trajectory and beamforming parameters are jointly optimized under power and mobility constraints, while satisfying communication requirements to the downlink user. To address the resulting non-convex problem, we employ null-space projection for beamforming design and adopt deep reinforcement learning for the trajectory optimization over a discrete-time scale. In each time slot, beamforming is optimized based on the channel state information to improve CRB performance while mitigating interference between the BS and the communication user. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces the time-averaged CRB by over 10%, compared with the ISAC system without UAV assistance, and also achieves a higher sensing accuracy than both the fixed-UAV-trajectory and the maximum-ratio-transmission-based beamforming benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [Optimal Break-Resilient Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19673)
*Canran Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种接近最优的二进制 break-resilient code，在 t≤n^(1−ε) 时实现 O(t log n) 冗余，达到信息论下界的常数因子范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有显式构造的冗余为 O(t log n log log log n)，而信息论下界为 Ω(t log(n/t))，两者仍有额外因子差距。论文目标是在断裂数 t 较大范围内缩小并基本闭合该差距。

Method: 核心方法是为消息计算一个短的代数指纹；解码时利用该指纹检验由接收碎片重新拼接得到的候选消息，从而拒绝错误组装。

Result: 当 t≤n^(1−ε) 且 ε∈(0,1) 固定时，构造出冗余为 O(t log n) 的 break-resilient code。

Conclusion: 该构造在指定参数范围内将冗余提升到与信息论下界相差常数因子的水平，显著改进了此前最优显式构造。

Abstract: Break-resilient codes protect a word against an omniscient adversary who breaks it at arbitrary boundaries between adjacent symbols. For binary codewords of length~$n$ subject to at most~$t$ breaks, the best known explicit construction for this model has redundancy~$O(t\log n\log\log\log n)$, whereas the information-theoretic lower bound is~$Ω(t\log (n/t))$. In this paper, we close this gap by presenting a break-resilient code with redundancy~$O(t\log n)$ when~$t\leq n^{1-\varepsilon}$ for a fixed $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, matching the information-theoretic lower bound up to a constant factor. The key idea is to compute a short algebraic fingerprint of the message, which enables the decoder to reject incorrect assemblies of the received fragments.

</details>


### [179] [Investigation of STEEP for Secure Communications Over SIMO and MISO Channels Subject to Full-Duplex Jamming and Eavesdropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19729)
*Md Saydur Rahman,Yingbo Hua*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在强窃听与全双工干扰条件下，STEEP方案用于SIMO/MISO无线安全通信的可达保密速率，并证明在一定功率条件下可保持正保密速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SIMO/MISO物理层安全通信在面对具有多天线、可全双工同时窃听与干扰的强对手时性能受限；因此需要分析新型STEEP方案在重度窃听信道中的安全通信能力。

Method: 论文考虑两种STEEP传输形式：第一阶段使用SIMO、第二阶段使用MISO，以及相反配置；在假设对手采用最优干扰与窃听策略的条件下，推导两种形式的可达保密速率，并与传统SIMO/MISO方案在随机衰落信道和有限发射功率下进行比较。

Result: 对于任意给定节点间信道条件和对手任意有限干扰功率，只要第一阶段发射功率为正且第二阶段功率足够大，两种STEEP形式均可实现正保密速率。数值/理论比较表明其与传统SIMO/MISO方案存在明确联系，并在强窃听场景下具备优势。

Conclusion: STEEP通过回显加密探测信号的机制增强了SIMO/MISO信道下的物理层安全性，尤其适用于面对多天线全双工窃听干扰者的重度威胁环境；其关键优势在于可在有限干扰功率下保证正保密速率。

Abstract: Secure communications over SIMO and MISO channels between wireless nodes are commonly encountered in applications and widely considered in the literature. This paper investigates such a problem by considering a newly proposed scheme called secrecy-message transmission by echoing encrypted probes (STEEP). We focus on a type of heavily eavesdropped channels where an aggressive eavesdropper (Eve) has multiple antennas and is capable of jamming and eavesdropping in the full-duplex mode. Assuming optimal jamming and eavesdropping by Eve, we present the secrecy rates achievable by STEEP in two forms: using SIMO channel in phase 1 and using MISO channel in phase 2, and vice versa. With any given channel condition between every pair of nodes and any finite jamming power by Eve, the secrecy rate of STEEP in either form is shown to be positive subject to a positive power in phase 1 and a sufficiently large power in phase 2. The connections between STEEP in either form and the conventional SIMO and MISO schemes are provided, and their secrecy rates are compared comprehensively subject to random fading channels and finite transmit powers.

</details>


### [180] [Optimization of MIMO STEEP for Secure Communications over MIMOME Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19738)
*Dinglin Yu,Yingbo Hua*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究将 STEEP 安全通信方案扩展到多天线 MIMO/MIMOME 信道，并通过优化预编码器显著提升可达保密速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理层安全方法在窃听信道强于合法用户信道时往往难以保证正保密速率；STEEP 通过“回显加密探测”在非对称功率分配条件下仍可能实现正保密速率。本文动机是进一步探索在多天线用户与多天线窃听者场景下，如何利用空间维度和预编码优化提升 STEEP 的安全传输性能。

Method: 作者将 STEEP 应用于 MIMO/MIMOME 信道，建立面向可达保密速率的预编码器优化问题；通过对多天线发送、接收以及窃听信道结构进行联合建模，优化合法用户侧的预编码策略，以增强合法链路有效信息传输并抑制窃听端可获得的信息。

Result: 研究表明，相比未优化或基准设计，经过优化的 MIMO STEEP 预编码器能够显著提高可达保密速率；该提升在窃听信道较强的情况下尤其重要。

Conclusion: 本文首次揭示了预编码优化对 MIMO STEEP 的关键作用，说明 STEEP 与 MIMO 空间处理结合后可在更严苛的窃听环境中获得更高安全通信性能。

Abstract: A transmission scheme for secure communications, called secret-message transmission by echoing encrypted probes (STEEP), can deliver a positive secrecy rate, even when eavesdropping channels are much stronger than that between users, subject to sufficient asymmetric power allocations from users. This paper considers an optimization problem for STEEP applied to MIMO (or MIMOME) channels between multi-antenna users and multi-antenna Eve. We reveal for the first time a significant increase of the achievable secrecy rate of MIMO STEEP using optimized precoders.

</details>


### [181] [Duality and Reverse Self-Dual Constructions for Hyperderivative Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19905)
*Hongchang Li,Zhihao Zhu,Weijun Fang,Jun Zhang,Sihuang Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究HRS码在欧氏内积下的对偶结构，给出显式分量表示，并据此刻画与构造反序自对偶HRS码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义Reed-Solomon码的欧氏对偶仍是广义Reed-Solomon码；但HRS码作为导数求值扩展，其对偶是否仍保持同类结构尚需明确。论文旨在解决HRS码对应的对偶性问题。

Method: 作者采用留数理论方法推导HRS码欧氏对偶的逐分量显式公式，并分析其与反序HRS求值码之间的块上三角等价关系；进一步利用该反序对偶结构研究反序自对偶条件。

Result: 结果表明，一般情况下HRS码的欧氏对偶并不是HRS码，而是与一个在每个求值块内对超导数阶数反序排列的HRS求值码块上三角等价。对于低重数的全域HRS码，这些三角变换可退化为对角缩放，对偶可由某个HRS码的行反序得到。论文还给出了反序自对偶HRS码的显式判据，并基于加法与乘法陪集结构构造了若干族实例。

Conclusion: 该工作澄清了HRS码欧氏对偶的真实结构，指出其通常不封闭于HRS码类，但具有可控的反序等价形式；这一结构进一步支持了反序自对偶HRS码的判定与系统构造。

Abstract: Hyperderivative Reed-Solomon (HRS) codes form a class of maximum-distance-separable codes under the Niederreiter-Rosenbloom-Tsfasman metric and may be viewed as a derivative-evaluation extension of classical Reed-Solomon codes. For generalized Reed-Solomon codes, the Euclidean dual is again a generalized Reed-Solomon code. In this paper, we investigate the corresponding duality problem for HRS codes. Using a residue-theoretic argument, we derive an explicit component-wise representation for the Euclidean dual of an HRS code. The formula shows that, in general, the Euclidean dual is not an HRS code. Instead, it is blockwise upper-triangularly equivalent to a reverse-order HRS evaluation code, where the reversal occurs in the hyperderivative orders within each evaluation block. In particular, for full-domain HRS codes with low multiplicity, the triangular transformations reduce to diagonal scalings, and the Euclidean dual is obtained as the row reversal of an HRS code. Based on this reverse-order dual structure, we further study reverse self-dual HRS codes. We establish explicit criteria for reverse self-duality and construct several families from additive and multiplicative coset structures.

</details>


### [182] [Spatial Semantic Communication: When Semantic Transmission Meets Index Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19934)
*Xinghao Guo,Yin Xu,Dazhi He,Hanjiang Hong,Zhiyong Chen,Cixiao Zhang,Yiyan Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合流体天线索引调制（FA-IM）与语义通信的空间语义通信系统，通过残差量化将连续语义特征离散化，并利用语义感知流拆分提升关键语义信息的传输可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字语义通信多依赖传统星座调制以保持兼容性，但在频谱效率与能量效率方面存在提升空间。索引调制能够利用额外维度承载信息，具备更高效率，因此有必要将其与语义通信结合。

Method: 论文提出兼容现有联合源信道编码（JSCC）框架的SSC系统：首先采用残差量化（RQ）将模拟语义特征转换为可数字传输的离散表示；随后利用FA-IM进行空间维度上的索引调制传输；同时设计语义感知流拆分机制，使更关键的语义信息分配到衰落更轻的信道路径。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该系统能够同时发挥RQ的高保真表示能力、语义感知拆分的可靠性优势以及FA-IM的空间效率，在数字语义传输中取得更稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作有效弥合了索引调制与语义通信之间的技术空白，提出的SSC框架为未来高效、可靠的数字语义通信提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Current digital semantic communication systems have primarily focused on maintaining compatibility with conventional constellation-based modulation. In contrast, index modulation (IM) represents a more spectrally and energy-efficient alternative by exploiting additional dimensions for information conveyance. Recognizing this potential, this paper bridges the gap between IM and semantic communications by proposing a novel spatial semantic communication (SSC) system leveraging cutting-edge fluid antenna-IM (FA-IM) technology. Compatible with existing joint source-channel coding (JSCC) architectures, the proposed SSC system employs the residual quantization (RQ) approach to discretize analog semantic features for subsequent digital IM transmission. Notably, the proposed SSC system synergizes RQ and IM via a semantic-aware stream splitting scheme, which ensures that critical semantic information undergoes less severe channel fading, thereby further optimizing semantic transmission performance. Simulation results validate that the proposed SSC system effectively integrates the high fidelity of RQ, the reliability of semantic-aware splitting, and the spatial efficiency of FA-IM, thereby providing a robust solution for future digital semantic transmission. The open source code is available at: https://github.com/gxh1106/SSC.

</details>


### [183] [On Runlength Limited Codes for BICM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20026)
*Stephan Zeitz,Florian Roth,Meik Dörpinghaus,Gerhard Fettweis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在BICM系统中优化RLL块码的比特到RLL符号分配，以提升低SNR和粗量化信道下的可达率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLL码可缓解粗量化系统中的符号间干扰，但其作为BICM符号映射器时，输入比特与RLL符号的分配方式会显著影响性能；同时信道记忆和1-bit量化非线性使信息论分析困难，因此需要可分析的模型与优化准则。

Method: 作者构建了一个带1-bit模数转换的块信道模型，用于描述单个RLL码块的传输；以BICM容量为指标，分析其与RLL分配方案的关系；在低SNR条件下推导最优分配方案对应的优化问题，并通过大块长渐近分析提出适用于有限块长和存在块间干扰信道的实用优化策略；进一步将方法扩展到两状态RLL码。

Result: 优化后的RLL分配方案能够提升BICM系统性能；在两状态RLL码场景中，优化TS-RLL码相比已有文献方案表现出显著性能改进，同时保持较高码率优势。

Conclusion: RLL码在BICM系统中的符号分配是影响系统性能的关键因素。通过低SNR下的BICM容量优化和渐近分析，可以设计出适用于实际有限块长系统的高效分配策略，尤其对TS-RLL码具有明显收益。

Abstract: We study the use of runlength-limited (RLL) block codes in bit-interleaved coded modulation (BICM) systems. In this setting, the RLL code acts as the symbol mapper, whose assignment between input bits and RLL symbols is critical for performance. In this work, we aim at optimizing the assignment scheme of RLL codes. One of the main applications of RLL codes is the mitigation of intersymbol interference (ISI) in systems with coarse quantization. However, channel memory and quantization nonlinearity complicate information theoretic analysis. To enable analytical treatment, we consider a block channel with 1-bit analog-to-digital conversion, modeling the transmission of a single RLL code block. For this channel, we investigate the relationship between the achievable rate in BICM systems-termed BICM capacity-and the RLL code's assignment scheme. Focusing on low signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), we derive the optimization problem yielding the optimal assignment scheme. By looking at asymptotically large block-lengths, we infer a practical optimization strategy for RLL codes with finite block-length and channels with inter-block interference. Further, we extend this optimization to two-state RLL (TS-RLL) codes, which offer higher code rates than state independent RLL codes. We demonstrate that optimized TS-RLL codes exhibit significant performance improvements over literature counterparts.

</details>


### [184] [Learning to Decode Quantum LDPC Codes via Cluster-Based Sequential Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20130)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Rémi A. Chou,David G. M. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向量子低密度奇偶校验码（QLDPC）的簇级强化学习顺序调度BP译码方法，在保持大部分VN级学习调度误码率收益的同时，显著减少每轮BP迭代中的调度决策次数，改善延迟与并行性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BP译码复杂度低但受短环、退化性和收敛失败影响；已有RL-S通过学习状态相关的变量节点更新顺序提升性能，但逐变量节点更新限制了迭代内并行性，导致延迟较高。

Method: 将变量节点划分为固定簇；每次调度由RL智能体选择一个簇，并行更新该簇内所有变量节点。为控制状态空间，提出基于局部不匹配权重归一化直方图并量化的置换不变簇状态表示，并构建簇级MDP、奖励函数与Q-learning更新规则。

Result: 在代表性QLDPC码上的数值实验表明，簇级学习调度保留了VN级学习顺序调度的大部分误码率改善，同时大幅降低每轮BP迭代所需的调度决策数量。

Conclusion: 该方法在译码性能、调度开销和并行执行能力之间提供了有吸引力的折中，适合需要低延迟并行译码的QLDPC应用场景。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes is attractive due to its low complexity, but its performance is often limited by short cycles, degeneracy, and convergence failures. Recently, reinforcement-learning-based sequential variable-node (VN) scheduling (RL-S) was shown to improve BP decoding by learning state-dependent update orders. However, the VN-by-VN nature of that approach offers limited within-iteration parallelism, since only one VN is updated at a time. In this paper, we propose a cluster-based extension of RL-S for QLDPC codes. The VNs are partitioned into fixed clusters, and at each scheduling step the RL agent selects one cluster to update, after which all VNs in that cluster are updated in parallel using the same pre-update incoming messages. To keep the tabular state space practical for large cluster sizes, we introduce a permutation-invariant cluster state based on a normalized histogram of local mismatch weights, followed by quantization. This representation makes the number of cluster states depend on the quantization resolution rather than the cluster size. We also develop the corresponding cluster-level Markov decision process, reward function, and Q-learning update. Numerical results on representative QLDPC codes show that the proposed clustered learned scheduling preserves most of the error-rate benefit of VN-level learned sequential scheduling while substantially reducing the number of scheduling decisions per BP iteration, thereby providing an attractive latency-parallelism tradeoff.

</details>


### [185] [Two-Way Wiretap Channel under Mixed Secrecy Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20172)
*Yanling Chen,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究双向窃听信道在强单边混合保密约束下的可达速率区域，提出非自适应与有限轮自适应密钥交换构造，获得指数级误差与泄漏界，并证明自适应区域包含非自适应方案且可能严格更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有双向窃听信道研究多关注双边保密或弱保密准则；本文关注更细化的单边保密场景，即仅要求用户1消息对外部监听者保密，同时用户2消息不受保密约束，并希望在强保密准则下给出可达区域与可靠性、泄漏的定量保证。

Method: 采用非自适应编码构造和对密钥交换构造的一侧化约简；针对固定自适应轮数建立指数误差与信息泄漏界；通过定量一次一密论证传播下一轮所用密钥的泄漏控制；使用显式填充构造消除初始化速率损失，并推导强混合保密可达区域。

Result: 非自适应构造在强保密准则下恢复了此前弱单边单字母内界；总体自适应可达区域包含非自适应构造作为回退方案，并包含一个可能严格更大的密钥交换子区域；对足够大块长可避免初始化速率损失。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在双向窃听信道的单边强混合保密设置中，有限轮自适应与密钥交换机制能够在严格泄漏控制下扩大可达区域，而非自适应方案仍提供稳健的基线内界。

Abstract: This paper studies the two-way wiretap channel (TW-WC) with an external eavesdropper under strong one-sided (mixed) secrecy: only User~1's message is required to be secure from the eavesdropper, while no secrecy constraint is imposed on User~2's message. Secrecy is measured by the information leakage of User~1's message to the eavesdropper. Applying a non-adaptive construction and a one-sided reduction of the key-exchange construction, we obtain exponential error and leakage bounds for every fixed number of adaptive rounds. A quantitative one-time-pad argument propagates the leakage of the key used in the next round, and an explicit padding construction removes the initialization-rate loss for all sufficiently large blocklengths. We derive the corresponding strong mixed-secrecy achievable regions. The non-adaptive construction recovers the previously reported weak one-sided single-letter inner bound under the stronger secrecy criterion. The overall adaptive achievable region includes the non-adaptive construction as a fallback and also contains a key-exchange subregion that can be strictly larger.

</details>


### [186] [Fundamental Limits of MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM in High-Dynamics ISAC: An Antenna Array Architecture Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20200)
*Po-Chih Chen,Ming-Chun Lee,Yu-Chih Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文比较高移动场景下 MIMO-OTFS 与 MIMO-OFDM ISAC 系统中稀疏阵列（SA）和均匀线阵（ULA）的通信容量与角度估计极限，结论是 SA 在空间维度上通常优于 ULA，而理想条件下波形差异对基本极限影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速移动场景会引入显著多普勒频移和快速时变信道，使通信与感知系统面临容量下降、角度估计精度受限等问题。论文希望明确在 MIMO-ISAC 中，调制波形（OTFS/OFDM）与阵列几何（SA/ULA）分别对系统基本性能极限的影响。

Method: 论文建立统一理论框架，分析遍历信道容量与角度估计的 Cramér–Rao Bound（CRB）。通信方面使用随机多数化框架刻画空间特征值分布对容量的影响；感知方面推导角度 CRB 与天线位置二阶矩之间的渐近关系，并比较 SA 与 ULA 的物理孔径和空间去相关能力。

Result: 研究表明，SA 通过形成更均匀的空间特征值分布降低多径相关性，从而提升通信容量；在感知方面，角度 CRB 渐近上与阵列天线位置二阶矩成反比，SA 因物理孔径更大可获得更高角度估计精度，提升幅度最高可达天线数平方量级。理想条件下，MIMO-OTFS 与 MIMO-OFDM 在容量和角度估计基本极限上表现相近。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是，在所研究的理想化高移动 MIMO-ISAC 场景中，空间阵列几何比波形选择更能决定通信与感知的基本性能增益；SA 相比传统 ULA 具有更优的容量和角度估计潜力。

Abstract: This paper investigates the fundamental limits of MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) systems in high-mobility environments, specifically comparing sparse arrays (SA) against conventional uniform linear arrays (ULA). High-dynamics scenarios, such as V2X and satellite networks, suffer from severe Doppler shifts and rapidly time-varying channels, necessitating robust modulation schemes and efficient array geometries. A unified theoretical analysis of ergodic channel capacity and the Cramér$\unicode{x2013}$Rao bound (CRB) for angle estimation is provided. Utilizing the framework of stochastic majorization, the study reveals that SAs consistently outperform ULAs by creating a more $\unicode{x201C}$uniform$\unicode{x201D}$ spatial eigenvalue distribution, which decorrelates the multipath environment and increases communication capacity. For sensing, the paper proves that the angle CRB is inversely proportional to the array's second-order moment of antenna positions asymptotically, demonstrating that SAs achieve superior accuracy$\unicode{x2014}$improving by up to the square of the number of antennas$\unicode{x2014}$due to their increased physical aperture. Notably, the analysis shows that under relatively ideal conditions, MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM share similar fundamental limits for both capacity and angle estimation, suggesting that spatial geometry, rather than waveform, is the primary driver of fundamental performance gains in the spatial dimension.

</details>


### [187] [Mapped ADMM: A Robust Algorithm for 1-Bit mMIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20414)
*Mohammad Amin Keshmiri,Masoud Ardakani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于去中心化 SVM 与共识 ADMM 的一比特大规模 MIMO 检测方法，通过分组分类器与有效星座点约束提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，一比特大规模 MIMO 检测可等价为二分类问题，并可用 SVM 高效求解；但传统集中式 SVM 在鲁棒性、共识融合以及实际检测性能方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 作者首先将 SVM 重构为由多个分类器组成的去中心化形式，使其可采用共识交替方向乘子法（CADMM）进行优化。随后进一步修改 CADMM，使输出被限制为有效星座点。同时，通过调整分组分类器规模，在共识精度与每组数据量所带来的分类器鲁棒性之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验或仿真表明，该方法在一比特大规模 MIMO 检测任务中显著优于现有实际可行的方法，检测性能得到明显提升。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将一比特 mMIMO 检测建模为去中心化 SVM 并结合改进 CADMM 的有效性；合理的分类器分组和星座点约束是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Recently, it has been reported that one-bit massive MIMO (mMIMO) detection is equivalent to a binary classification problem that can be solved efficiently using support vector machine (SVM). Inspired by this result, we first reformulate SVM in a decentralized form consisting of multiple classifiers. This enables the use of the consensus alternating direction method of multipliers (CADMM), a technique that can improve robustness and performance through its inherent consensus making. We further update CADMM to output only valid constellation points and achieve significantly improved detection performance. In our method, by changing the size of the grouped classifiers, we balance the number of classifiers for consensus accuracy with sufficient data per group to ensure classifier robustness. Ultimately, we demonstrate that our proposed method significantly outperforms existing practically feasible methods for one-bit mMIMO detection.

</details>
